slower than usual during the summer holiday period. Normal service will resume after the summer holidays, but things may remain a bit slow until the beginning of August.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat is Backstage?\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-what-is-backstage\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What is Backstage?\" href=\"#what-is-backstage\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://backstage.io/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eBackstage\u003c/a\u003e is an open source framework for building developer portals. Powered by a centralized software catalog, Backstage restores order to your microservices and infrastructure and enables your product teams to ship high-quality code quickly without compromising autonomy.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBackstage unifies all your infrastructure tooling, services, and documentation to create a streamlined development environment from end to end.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/backstage/backstage/blob/master/docs/assets/header.png\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"/backstage/backstage/raw/master/docs/assets/header.png\" alt=\"software-catalog\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOut of the box, Backstage includes:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://backstage.io/docs/features/software-catalog/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eBackstage Software Catalog\u003c/a\u003e for managing all your software such as microservices, libraries, data pipelines, websites, and ML models\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://backstage.io/docs/features/software-templates/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eBackstage Software Templates\u003c/a\u003e for quickly spinning up new projects and standardizing your tooling with your organization’s best practices\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://backstage.io/docs/features/techdocs/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eBackstage TechDocs\u003c/a\u003e for making it easy to create, maintain, find, and use technical documentation, using a \"docs like code\" approach\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePlus, a growing ecosystem of \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/backstage/backstage/tree/master/plugins\"\u003eopen source plugins\u003c/a\u003e that further expand Backstage’s customizability and functionality\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBackstage was created by Spotify but is now hosted by the \u003ca href=\"https://www.cncf.io\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eCloud Native Computing Foundation (CNCF)\u003c/a\u003e as an Incubation level project. For more information, see the \u003ca href=\"https://backstage.io/blog/2022/03/16/backstage-turns-two#out-of-the-sandbox-and-into-incubation\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eannouncement\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eProject <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What is Backstage?</h2><a id="user-content-what-is-backstage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What is Backstage?" href="#what-is-backstage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://backstage.io/" rel="nofollow">Backstage</a> is an open source framework for building developer portals. Powered by a centralized software catalog, Backstage restores order to your microservices and infrastructure and enables your product teams to ship high-quality code quickly without compromising autonomy.</p>
<p dir="auto">Backstage unifies all your infrastructure tooling, services, and documentation to create a streamlined development environment from end to end.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/backstage/backstage/blob/master/docs/assets/header.png"><img src="/backstage/backstage/raw/master/docs/assets/header.png" alt="software-catalog" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Out of the box, Backstage includes:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://backstage.io/docs/features/software-catalog/" rel="nofollow">Backstage Software Catalog</a> for managing all your software such as microservices, libraries, data pipelines, websites, and ML models</li>
<li><a href="https://backstage.io/docs/features/software-templates/" rel="nofollow">Backstage Software Templates</a> for quickly spinning up new projects and standardizing your tooling with your organization’s best practices</li>
<li><a href="https://backstage.io/docs/features/techdocs/" rel="nofollow">Backstage TechDocs</a> for making it easy to create, maintain, find, and use technical documentation, using a "docs like code" approach</li>
<li>Plus, a growing ecosystem of <a href="https://github.com/backstage/backstage/tree/master/plugins">open source plugins</a> that further expand Backstage’s customizability and functionality</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Backstage was created by Spotify but is now hosted by the <a href="https://www.cncf.io" rel="nofollow">Cloud Native Computing Foundation (CNCF)</a> as an Incubation level project. For more information, see the <a href="https://backstage.io/blog/2022/03/16/backstage-turns-two#out-of-the-sandbox-and-into-incubation" rel="nofollow">announcement</a>.</p>

<p dir="auto">To engage with our community, you can use the following resources:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://discord.gg/backstage-687207715902193673" rel="nofollow">Discord chatroom</a> - Get support or discuss the project</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/backstage/backstage/blob/master/CONTRIBUTING.md">Contributing to Backstage</a> - Start here if you want to contribute</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/backstage/backstage/labels/rfc">RFCs</a> - Help shape the technical direction</li>
<li><a href="https://backstage.io/docs/faq" rel="nofollow">FAQ</a> - Frequently Asked Questions</li>
<li><a href="/backstage/backstage/blob/master/CODE_OF_CONDUCT.md">Code of Conduct</a> - This is how we roll</li>
<li><a href="/backstage/backstage/blob/master/ADOPTERS.md">Adopters</a> - Companies already using Backstage</li>
<li><a href="https://backstage.io/blog/" rel="nofollow">Blog</a> - Announcements and updates</li>
<li><a href="https://spoti.fi/backstagenewsletter" rel="nofollow">Newsletter</a> - Subscribe to our email newsletter</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/backstage/community">Backstage Community Sessions</a> - Join monthly meetups and explore Backstage community</li>
<li>Give us a star ⭐️ - If you are using Backstage or think it is an interesting project, we would love a star ❤️</li>
</ul>

        data-analytics-event="{&quot;location&quot;:&quot;footer&quot;,&quot;action&quot;:&quot;dont_share_info&quot;,&quot;context&quot;:&quot;subfooter&quot;,&quot;tag&quot;:&quot;link&quot;,&quot;label&quot;:&quot;dont_share_info_link_subfooter_footer&quot;}"
      >
        Do not share my personal information
      </button>
    </cookie-consent-link>
  </li>

      </ul>
    </nav>
  </div>
</footer>



    <ghcc-consent id="ghcc" class="position-fixed bottom-0 left-0" style="z-index: 999999"
      data-locale="en"
      data-initial-cookie-consent-allowed=""
      data-cookie-consent-required="false"
    ></ghcc-consent>
